┬╖     SELECT product_code,
┬╖            SUM(CAST(qty AS DOUBLE) * CAST(unit_price AS DOUBLE)) AS Rev
┬╖     FROM 'cleaned_consolidated_sales.csv'

┬╖ )

D WITH StoreProductSales AS (
┬╖     SELECT StoreID,
┬╖            product_code,
┬╖            SUM(CAST(qty AS DOUBLE) * CAST(unit_price AS DOUBLE)) AS Rev,
┬╖            ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY StoreID ORDER BY SUM(CAST(qty AS DOUBLE) * CAST(unit_price AS DOUBLE)) DESC) as rnk
┬╖     FROM 'cleaned_consolidated_sales.csv'
┬╖     WHERE product_code NOT IN ('TAX', 'TENDER', 'DISC')
┬╖     GROUP BY StoreID, product_code
┬╖ )
┬╖ SELECT StoreID,
┬╖        product_code,
┬╖        ROUND(Rev, 2) AS Revenue
┬╖ FROM StoreProductSales
┬╖ WHERE rnk <= 3
┬╖ ORDER BY StoreID, rnk;

memory D CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS staging_sales AS
         SELECT * EXCLUDE (ts),
                CASE
                    -- If ts is a numeric epoch string, convert it from seconds
                    WHEN ts GLOB '[0-9]*' THEN to_timestamp(CAST(ts AS BIGINT))
                    -- Otherwise, parse it as a standard timestamp string
                    ELSE TRY_CAST(ts AS TIMESTAMP)
                END AS ts
         FROM read_csv('sales/*.csv', union_by_name := true, header := true, types := {'ts': 'VARCHAR'});

memory D CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE fact_sales AS
         WITH deduplicated AS (
             SELECT *,
                    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
                        PARTITION BY bill_no, product_code, ts, store_id
                        -- keep the latest ingested record if exact duplicates exist
                        ORDER BY ts DESC
                    ) as rn
             FROM staging_sales
         )
         SELECT * EXCLUDE (rn)
         FROM deduplicated
         WHERE rn = 1;

memory D CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE fact_sales AS
         WITH normalized AS (
             SELECT
                 -- Extract StoreID from the filename (e.g., SALES_S01_20240101.csv -> S01)
                 regexp_extract(file, 'SALES_(S[0-9]+)_', 1) AS store_id,
                 COALESCE(bill_no) AS bill_no,
                 COALESCE(product_code, item_code) AS product_code,
                 COALESCE(qty, quantity, 0) AS qty,
                 COALESCE(unit_price, rate, 0.0) AS unit_price,
                 COALESCE(line_type, type) AS line_type,
                 ts,
                 ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
                     PARTITION BY
                         regexp_extract(file, 'SALES_(S[0-9]+)_', 1),
                         COALESCE(bill_no),
                         COALESCE(product_code, item_code),
                         ts
                     ORDER BY ts DESC
                 ) as rn
             FROM staging_sales
         )
         SELECT store_id, bill_no, product_code, qty, unit_price, line_type, ts
         FROM normalized
         WHERE rn = 1;
Binder Error:
Referenced column "file" not found in FROM clause!

memory D CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE fact_sales AS
         WITH raw_data AS (
             SELECT *, filename AS file_path
             FROM read_csv('sales/*.csv', union_by_name := true, header := true, filename := true, types := {'ts': 'VARCHAR'})
         ),
         normalized AS (
             SELECT
                 regexp_extract(file_path, 'SALES_(S[0-9]+)_', 1) AS store_id,
                 COALESCE(bill_no) AS bill_no,
                 COALESCE(product_code, item_code) AS product_code,
                 COALESCE(qty, quantity, 0) AS qty,
                 COALESCE(unit_price, rate, 0.0) AS unit_price,
                 COALESCE(line_type, type) AS line_type,
                 CASE
                     WHEN regexp_matches(ts, '^[0-9]+$') THEN to_timestamp(CAST(ts AS BIGINT))
                     ELSE TRY_CAST(ts AS TIMESTAMP)
                 END AS ts,
                 ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
                     PARTITION BY
                         regexp_extract(file_path, 'SALES_(S[0-9]+)_', 1),
                         COALESCE(bill_no),
                         COALESCE(product_code, item_code),
                         ts
                     ORDER BY ts DESC
                 ) as rn
             FROM raw_data
         )
         SELECT store_id, bill_no, product_code, qty, unit_price, line_type, ts
         FROM normalized
         WHERE rn = 1;
memory D SELECT
             COUNT(*) AS total_rows,
             md5(string_agg(hash(store_id || bill_no || product_code || CAST(ts AS VARCHAR)), ',' ORDER BY store_id, bill_no, product_code, ts)) AS table_checksum
         FROM fact_sales;

memory D WITH computed_monthly AS (
             SELECT
                 strftime(ts, '%Y-%m') AS month,
                 ROUND(SUM(qty * unit_price), 2) AS computed_rev
             FROM fact_sales
             GROUP BY month
         )
         SELECT
             c.month,
             c.computed_rev,
             f.revenue AS target_rev,
             ROUND(ABS(c.computed_rev - f.revenue), 2) AS variance
         FROM computed_monthly c
         LEFT JOIN read_csv('finance_monthly.csv', header := true) f ON c.month = f.month
         ORDER BY c.month;

memory D WITH computed_monthly AS (
             SELECT
                 strftime(ts, '%Y-%m') AS month,
                 ROUND(SUM(qty * unit_price), 2) AS computed_rev
             FROM fact_sales
             GROUP BY month
         )
         SELECT
             c.month,
             c.computed_rev,
             f.revenue_inr AS target_rev,
             ROUND(ABS(c.computed_rev - f.revenue_inr), 2) AS variance
         FROM computed_monthly c
         LEFT JOIN read_csv('finance_monthly.csv', header := true) f ON c.month = f.month
         ORDER BY c.month;

memory D SELECT
             st.store_name,
             c.category_name,
             strftime(f.ts, '%A') AS day_of_week,
             strftime(f.ts, '%Y-%m') AS report_month,
             SUM(f.qty * f.unit_price) AS total_revenue
         FROM fact_sales f
         JOIN dim_stores st ON f.store_sk = st.store_sk
         JOIN products p ON f.product_sk = p.product_sk
         JOIN dim_categories c ON p.category_id = c.category_id
         GROUP BY
             st.store_name,
             c.category_name,
             day_of_week,
             report_month
         ORDER BY report_month, st.store_name;

└────────────┴───────────────┴─────────────┴──────────────┴───────────────┘

memory D SELECT
             strftime(s.ts, '%Y-%m') AS report_month,
             SUM(s.qty * p.mrp) AS total_revenue
         FROM fact_sales s
         JOIN products dp ON s.product_code = dp.product_code
         ASOF JOIN price_revisions p
             ON dp.product_sk = p.product_sk
             AND s.ts >= p.effective_from
         WHERE strftime(s.ts, '%Y-%m') IN ('2024-03', '2024-10')
         GROUP BY report_month;

memory D -- Create a temporary or staging table for your finance targets
memory D CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS finance_monthly AS
         SELECT * FROM read_csv_auto('finance_monthly.csv');
memory D
memory D -- Run the reconciliation comparison query
memory D SELECT
             strftime(s.ts, '%Y-%m') AS "Month",
             ROUND(SUM(s.qty * s.unit_price), 2) AS "Computed Revenue",
             ROUND(f.revenue_inr, 2) AS "Target Revenue (revenue_inr)",
             ROUND(SUM(s.qty * s.unit_price) - f.revenue_inr, 2) AS "Variance",
             'Definition Difference (Gross vs. Net / Tax / Discounts)' AS "Root Cause Category"
         FROM fact_sales s
         JOIN finance_monthly f ON strftime(s.ts, '%Y-%m') = f.month
         WHERE strftime(s.ts, '%Y-%m') IN ('2024-03', '2024-10')
         GROUP BY strftime(s.ts, '%Y-%m'), f.revenue_inr
         ORDER BY "Month";
